\title{The $\mathbb{Z}/9$ Vortex Framework: reflections, division-by-zero as
domain change, and a $7=6+1$ correspondence with the Clay Millennium Problems
(observation-based, recomputable)}
\author{Tsvetan Rouschev\\\small ORCID: \href{https://orcid.org/0009-0000-7312-9778}{0009-0000-7312-9778}}
\date{2026}

\newtheorem{theorem}{Theorem}[section]

\begin{document}
\maketitle

\begin{abstract}
We study a finite-arithmetic framework on the ring $\mathbb{Z}/9$ (the ``vortex'')
and the group $(\mathbb{Z}/7)^{*}$ (the seven-ray Pliska ``rosette''), from an
observation standpoint: each statement is computed, not asserted. Division by zero
is treated as a change of domain ($n/0 := n^{-1} \bmod 9$ on units; $0 \leftrightarrow
\infty$ on the Riemann sphere); inversion and the ten's-complement reflection
$10-d$ are involutions; a $7=6+1$ bijection maps the rosette (six units $+$ center)
onto the Clay set (six open problems $+$ Poincar\'e). A mechanical entailment test
reports \textbf{0/7}. \textbf{Observed conclusion:} the framework reflects/maps the
Millennium Problems into one algebraic structure; it does \emph{not} solve them,
and it is not a physical theory. It is a proof of concept and a classical simulator.
All results recompute from source (TypeScript; formalized in Lean~4).
\end{abstract}

\section{Scope}
This is not a resolution of the Clay Millennium Problems. The earlier
``$\exists\,\alpha,\ \top \wedge 1^2=1 \wedge 1=1$'' statements are tautologies:
true in a world where the conjecture is false, hence entailing nothing (0/7). What
is exact here is the $\mathbb{Z}/9$ / $(\mathbb{Z}/7)^{*}$ algebra, the reflections
and folds, and the geometry (cube $\to$ hexagon $\to$ Seed/Flower/Fruit/Metatron).

\section{Results (computed)}
\begin{itemize}
\item Units $(\mathbb{Z}/9)^{*}=\{1,2,4,5,7,8\}$; nilradical $\{0,3,6,9\}$ with
      $3^2\equiv 6^2\equiv 0$; doubling $\langle 2\rangle$ is $C_6$.
\item $10-d$ is an involution, fixed digit $5$; multiplicative inverses
      $2\leftrightarrow5$, $4\leftrightarrow7$.
\item $(\mathbb{Z}/7)^{*}\cong C_6 \cong (\mathbb{Z}/9)^{*}$; generator
      $3\ (\mathrm{mod}\ 7)\leftrightarrow 2\ (\mathrm{mod}\ 9)$.
\item Shell-model magic numbers $2,8,20,28,50,82,126$ (oscillator + spin-orbit);
      the $10\cdot 2^{k}$ ladder hits only the oscillator closures $20,40$.
\item Proton/electron ratio: fit $108\cdot 17 = 1836$ vs measured $1836.15267343$;
      $1836 \neq 1836.1527$ (curve-fit, not prediction).
\end{itemize}

\section{Reproducibility}
See the repository: \url{https://github.com/ceccec/millennium-solutions}. Fused
TypeScript modules recompute every result; Lean~4 theorems (\texttt{decide},
\texttt{norm\_num}) formalize the exact facts. License: CC BY-NC-ND 4.0.

\section{Theorems}

Every statement below is a declaration the Lean~4 kernel accepts, sorry-free; the exhaustions are axiom-free and the
proofs carry only the standard axioms \texttt{propext} and \texttt{Quot.sound}, and the
\LaTeX{} is generated from the Lean source and checked to read back symbol for symbol against it
(\texttt{npm run latex-gate}). There are 905 of them across 38 files and
7 wings; 704 close by exhaustion over a stated finite domain, 187 are proved
for every value, and the remaining 14 close by \texttt{rfl} and are counted separately, never as theorems.

\paragraph{\texttt{address.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{raw\_bytes\_of\_a}]
\[ \mathrm{rawBytes}\mathopen{}\left(A\right) = [88,\,118,\,248,\,251,\,63,\,149,\,14,\,202,\,10,\,251,\,189,\,97,\,221,\,134,\,206,\,204] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 16 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{to\_uuid\_bytes\_of\_a}]
\[ \mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(A\right) = [88,\,118,\,248,\,251,\,63,\,149,\,142,\,202,\,138,\,251,\,189,\,97,\,221,\,134,\,206,\,204] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 16 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{to\_uuid\_bytes\_of\_uuidna}]
\[ \mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{UUIDNA}\right) = [252,\,81,\,21,\,50,\,110,\,138,\,132,\,24,\,165,\,34,\,165,\,27,\,29,\,70,\,167,\,12] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 16 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{address\_is\_sixteen\_bytes}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{UUIDNA}\right)\right| = 16 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{address\_bytes\_are\_bytes}]
\[ \forall b \in \mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{UUIDNA}\right),\; b < 256 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{version\_nibble\_is\_forced}]
\[ \frac{\mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(A\right)_{6}}{16} = 8 \land \frac{\mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{UUIDNA}\right)_{6}}{16} = 8 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{variant\_bits\_are\_forced}]
\[ \frac{\mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(A\right)_{8}}{64} = 2 \land \frac{\mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{UUIDNA}\right)_{8}}{64} = 2 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{addressing\_is\_deterministic}]
\[ \mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(A\right) = \mathrm{toUuidBytes}\mathopen{}\left(A\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{distinct\_seeds\_give\_distinct\_addresses}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{addressing\_is\_injective\_on\_single\_characters}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{addressing\_is\_not\_the\_identity}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{addressing\_is\_not\_constant}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_version\_stamp\_keeps\_the\_low\_nibble\_of\_every\_byte}]
\texttt{∀ b : Nat, b < 256 → or8 (and8 b 15) 128 = 128 + b \% 16}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_variant\_stamp\_keeps\_the\_low\_six\_bits\_of\_every\_byte}]
\texttt{∀ b : Nat, b < 256 → or8 (and8 b 63) 128 = 128 + b \% 64}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{stamping\_twice\_is\_stamping\_once}]
\texttt{∀ b : Nat, b < 256 → or8 (and8 (or8 (and8 b 15) 128) 15) 128 = or8 (and8 b 15) 128 ∧ or8 (and8 (or8 (and8 b 63) 128) 63) 128 = or8 (and8 b 63) 128}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_third\_group\_is\_version\_eight\_over\_twelve\_hash\_bits}]
\texttt{∀ b6 b7 : Nat, b6 < 256 → b7 < 256 → (or8 (and8 b6 15) 128) * 256 + b7 = 8 * 4096 + (b6 \% 16 * 256 + b7) ∧ b6 \% 16 * 256 + b7 < 4096}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_fourth\_group\_is\_variant\_two\_over\_fourteen\_hash\_bits}]
\texttt{∀ b8 b9 : Nat, b8 < 256 → b9 < 256 → (or8 (and8 b8 63) 128) * 256 + b9 = 2 * 16384 + (b8 \% 64 * 256 + b9) ∧ b8 \% 64 * 256 + b9 < 16384}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_unstamped\_bits\_read\_back\_from\_every\_stamped\_byte}]
\texttt{∀ b : Nat, b < 256 → and8 (or8 (and8 b 15) 128) 15 = b \% 16 ∧ and8 (or8 (and8 b 63) 128) 63 = b \% 64}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_twelve\_hash\_bits\_read\_back\_from\_the\_third\_group}]
\texttt{∀ b6 b7 : Nat, b6 < 256 → b7 < 256 → ((or8 (and8 b6 15) 128) * 256 + b7) / 4096 = 8 ∧ ((or8 (and8 b6 15) 128) * 256 + b7) \% 4096 = b6 \% 16 * 256 + b7}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_fourteen\_hash\_bits\_read\_back\_from\_the\_fourth\_group}]
\texttt{∀ b8 b9 : Nat, b8 < 256 → b9 < 256 → ((or8 (and8 b8 63) 128) * 256 + b9) / 16384 = 2 ∧ ((or8 (and8 b8 63) 128) * 256 + b9) \% 16384 = b8 \% 64 * 256 + b9}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{asymmetric.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_signature\_is\_four\_uuids\_and\_a\_key\_is\_two}]
\[ \mathrm{signatureBytes} = 4 \cdot \mathrm{uuidBytes} \land \mathrm{publicKeyBytes} = 2 \cdot \mathrm{uuidBytes} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{neither\_the\_key\_nor\_the\_signature\_fits\_in\_a\_uuid}]
\[ \mathrm{uuidBytes} < \mathrm{publicKeyBytes} \land \mathrm{uuidBytes} < \mathrm{signatureBytes} \land \mathrm{uuidBytes} \cdot 8 < \mathrm{signatureBytes} \cdot 8 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{not\_even\_the\_whole\_uuid\_would\_hold\_one}]
\[ \mathrm{uuidBytes} \cdot 8 = 128 \land \mathrm{signatureBytes} \cdot 8 = 512 \land 128 < 512 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{what\_travels\_beside\_the\_identifier}]
\[ \mathrm{publicKeyBytes} + \mathrm{signatureBytes} = 96 \land 96 = 6 \cdot \mathrm{uuidBytes} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_check\_cannot\_separate\_the\_payloads}]
\[ 2^{\mathrm{checkBits}} < 2^{\mathrm{payloadBits}} \land \mathrm{payloadBits} = 90 \land \mathrm{checkBits} = 32 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_gap\_is\_fifty\_eight\_bits}]
\[ \mathrm{payloadBits} - \mathrm{checkBits} = 58 \land 2^{\mathrm{payloadBits}} = 2^{58} \cdot 2^{\mathrm{checkBits}} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_checksum\_is\_the\_same\_whoever\_computes\_it}]
\[ \forall s \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \forall t \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \forall p \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; \mathrm{checkOf}\mathopen{}\left(s,\,p\right) = \mathrm{checkOf}\mathopen{}\left(t,\,p\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 432 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{two\_secrets\_part\_company\_on\_every\_payload}]
\[ \forall p \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; \mathrm{tagOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,p\right) \neq \mathrm{tagOf}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,p\right) \land \lnot \forall s \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \forall t \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \forall p \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; \mathrm{tagOf}\mathopen{}\left(s,\,p\right) = \mathrm{tagOf}\mathopen{}\left(t,\,p\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 5,184 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_tag\_moves\_with\_the\_payload}]
\[ \forall p \in \{0,\dots,10\},\; \mathrm{tagOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,p\right) \neq \mathrm{tagOf}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,p + 1\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 11 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_square\_root\_rule\_is\_the\_one\_this\_prime\_admits}]
\[ p \bmod 8 = 5 \land p + 3 \bmod 8 = 0 \land p \bmod 4 = 1 \land p \bmod 4 \neq 3 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_cofactor\_is\_bracketed\_at\_eight}]
\[ 4 \cdot L < p \land p < 8 \cdot L \land L < p \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{asymmetric\_settles\_its\_range}]
\[ \mathrm{settledHere} = 11 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{rfl} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_proof\_is\_smaller\_than\_its\_set\_across\_the\_octave}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{exactly\_one\_digit\_reflects\_out\_of\_range}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_classes\_partition\_the\_nine}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{tetA}\right| + \left|\mathrm{tetB}\right| + \left|\mathrm{axis}\right| = 9 \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{tetA} \mathbin{+\!\!+} \mathrm{tetB} \mathbin{+\!\!+} \mathrm{axis}\right)\right| = 9 \land \left|\mathrm{digits}\right| = 10 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_digit\_is\_sorted\_exactly\_once}]
\[ \forall d \in \mathrm{digits},\; d \in \mathrm{tetB} \land \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \in \mathrm{tetB} \lor d \in \mathrm{tetA} \land \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \in \mathrm{axis} \lor d \in \mathrm{axis} \land \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \in \mathrm{tetA} \lor \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) = d \lor \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) > 9 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_fall\_fixes\_every\_digit\_but\_the\_void}]
\[ \{\, d \in \mathrm{digits} \mid \mathrm{fall}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \neq d \,\} = [0] \land \forall d \in \mathrm{nonzero},\; \mathrm{fall}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) = d \]

\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_fall\_and\_the\_reflection\_share\_one\_exceptional\_digit}]
\[ \{\, d \in \mathrm{digits} \mid \mathrm{fall}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \neq d \,\} = \{\, d \in \mathrm{digits} \mid \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) > 9 \,\} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{on\_the\_fixed\_points\_the\_reflection\_never\_leaves}]
\[ \forall d \in \mathrm{nonzero},\; \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \ge 1 \land \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \le 9 \land \forall d \in \mathrm{nonzero},\; \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right)\right) = d \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{what\_escapes\_falls\_back\_inside}]
\[ \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(0\right) = 10 \land \mathrm{fall}\mathopen{}\left(10\right) = 1 \land \mathrm{fall}\mathopen{}\left(10\right) \in \mathrm{nonzero} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_nines\_complement\_pairs\_the\_ten\_digits}]
\[ \forall d \in \mathrm{digits},\; 9 - d \in \mathrm{digits} \land 9 - 9 - d = d \land 9 - d \neq d \land \left|\{\, d \in \mathrm{digits} \mid d < 9 - d \,\}\right| \cdot 2 = \left|\mathrm{digits}\right| \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{elementary.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{euclids\_form\_is\_perfect\_at\_every\_mersenne\_prime\_to\_seven}]
\[ \forall p \in [2,\,3,\,5,\,7],\; \lnot \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(2^{p} - 1\right) \lor \mathrm{properDivisorSum}\mathopen{}\left(2^{p - 1} \cdot 2^{p} - 1\right) = 2^{p - 1} \cdot 2^{p} - 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 4 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_mersenne\_hypothesis\_is\_real\_at\_eleven}]
\[ \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(2^{11} - 1\right) = \mathrm{false} \land 23 \cdot 89 = 2^{11} - 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_amicable\_pair\_is\_mutual\_and\_neither\_is\_perfect}]
\[ \mathrm{properDivisorSum}\mathopen{}\left(220\right) = 284 \land \mathrm{properDivisorSum}\mathopen{}\left(284\right) = 220 \land \mathrm{properDivisorSum}\mathopen{}\left(220\right) \neq 220 \land \mathrm{properDivisorSum}\mathopen{}\left(284\right) \neq 284 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_primitive\_roots\_mod\_nine\_are\_exactly\_two\_and\_five}]
\[ \{\, g \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \mid \mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(g,\,9\right) = 6 \,\} = [2,\,5] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 2 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_eisenstein\_units\_are\_exactly\_six}]
\texttt{(((List.range 7).flatMap (fun (i : Nat) => (List.range 7).map (fun (j : Nat) => (((i : Int) - 3, (j : Int) - 3) : Int × Int)))).filter (fun p => eisensteinNorm p.1 p.2 == 1)).length = 6}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 49 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_subtraction\_game\_loses\_exactly\_at\_the\_multiples\_of\_four}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,40\},\; \begin{cases}\forall m \in [1,\,2,\,3],\; \lnot m \le n \lor n - m \bmod 4 \neq 0 & \text{if } n \bmod 4 = 0\\ \exists m \in [1,\,2,\,3] : m \le n \land n - m \bmod 4 = 0 & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 369 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_units\_of\_z9\_form\_three\_pairs\_summing\_to\_nine}]
\[ \left|\{\, u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \mid u < 9 - u \,\}\right| = 3 \land \forall u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; 9 - u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \land u + 9 - u = 9 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_multiplication\_table\_of\_the\_z9\_units\_is\_a\_latin\_square}]
\[ \forall a \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, a \cdot b \bmod 9 \mid b \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \,\}\right)\right| = \left|\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)\right| \land \forall x \in \{\, a \cdot b \bmod 9 \mid b \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \,\},\; x \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \land \forall b \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, a \cdot b \bmod 9 \mid a \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \,\}\right)\right| = \left|\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)\right| \land \forall x \in \{\, a \cdot b \bmod 9 \mid a \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \,\},\; x \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_count\_of\_primitive\_roots\_is\_phi\_of\_phi}]
\[ \forall n \in \{2,\dots,60\},\; \lnot \mathrm{hasPrimitiveRoot}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \lor \left|\{\, g \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \mid \mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(g,\,n\right) = \left|\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)\right| \,\}\right| = \mathrm{totient}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{totient}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 59 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_order\_spectrum\_of\_the\_z9\_units\_realises\_gauss\_divisor\_sum}]
\[ \forall d \in \{\, d \in \{1,\dots,6\} \mid 6 \bmod d = 0 \,\},\; \left|\{\, g \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \mid \mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(g,\,9\right) = d \,\}\right| = \mathrm{totient}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) \land \sum \mathrm{totient}[\{\, d \in \{1,\dots,6\} \mid 6 \bmod d = 0 \,\}] = 6 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 36 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_product\_of\_the\_units\_is\_minus\_one\_exactly\_where\_gauss\_says}]
\[ \forall n \in \{2,\dots,60\},\; \operatorname{fold}_{a,\,u \mapsto a \cdot u \bmod n}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(n\right),\, 1\right) = \begin{cases}n - 1 & \text{if } \mathrm{gaussCyclic}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)\\ 1 & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 59 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{three\_is\_a\_primitive\_root\_mod\_seven\_and\_seven\_is\_six\_plus\_one}]
\[ \mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(3,\,7\right) = 6 \land \left|\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(7\right)\right| = 6 \land \left|\{0,\dots,6\}\right| = \left|\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(7\right)\right| + 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 7 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_z9\_unit\_group\_splits\_as\_reflection\_times\_trinity}]
\[ \forall u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,3,\,9\right) \cdot \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,4,\,9\right) \bmod 9 = u \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,3,\,9\right) \mid u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \,\}\right)\right| = 2 \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,4,\,9\right) \mid u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \,\}\right)\right| = 3 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{an\_odd\_prime\_is\_a\_sum\_of\_two\_squares\_exactly\_when\_it\_is\_one\_mod\_four}]
\[ \mathrm{isSumOfTwoSquares}\mathopen{}\left(2\right) \land \forall p \in \{\, x \in \{3,\dots,199\} \mid \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\},\; \mathrm{isSumOfTwoSquares}\mathopen{}\left(p\right) = p \bmod 4 = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 197 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_area\_of\_an\_integer\_right\_triangle\_is\_a\_multiple\_of\_six}]
\[ \forall a \in \{1,\dots,40\},\; \forall b \in \{1,\dots,40\},\; \lnot \exists c \in \{1,\dots,60\} : a \cdot a + b \cdot b = c \cdot c \lor \frac{a \cdot b}{2} \bmod 6 = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 96,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_inverse\_map\_permutes\_the\_units\_and\_is\_an\_involution}]
\[ \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{invUnit}[\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)]\right)\right| = \left|\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)\right| \land \forall x \in \mathrm{invUnit}[\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)],\; x \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \land \forall u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; \mathrm{invUnit}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{invUnit}\mathopen{}\left(u\right)\right) = u \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_hockey\_stick\_identity\_holds\_across\_the\_range}]
\[ \forall r \in \{0,\dots,10\},\; \forall m \in \{0,\dots,18\},\; \sum \{\, \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(i,\,r\right) \mid i \in \{r,\dots,r+m + 1-1\} \,\} = \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(r + m + 1,\,r + 1\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 209 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{three\_is\_the\_only\_prime\_one\_less\_than\_a\_square\_in\_this\_range}]
\[ \{\, n \in \{2,\dots,41\} \mid \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(n \cdot n - 1\right) \,\} = [2] \land \forall n \in \{3,\dots,41\},\; n - 1 \cdot n + 1 = n \cdot n - 1 \land n - 1 > 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,560 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{eight\_times\_a\_triangular\_number\_plus\_one\_is\_an\_odd\_square}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,59\},\; 8 \cdot \frac{n \cdot n + 1}{2} + 1 = 2 \cdot n + 1 \cdot 2 \cdot n + 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 60 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_powers\_of\_two\_mod\_nine\_and\_of\_three\_mod\_seven\_are\_isomorphic}]
\[ \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,k,\,9\right) \mid k \in \{0,\dots,5\} \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(3,\,k,\,7\right) \mid k \in \{0,\dots,5\} \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \land \forall x \in \{\, \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,k,\,9\right) \mid k \in \{0,\dots,5\} \,\},\; x \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) \land \forall x \in \{\, \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(3,\,k,\,7\right) \mid k \in \{0,\dots,5\} \,\},\; x \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(7\right) \land \forall i \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \forall j \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,i,\,9\right) \cdot \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,j,\,9\right) \bmod 9 = \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,i + j \bmod 6,\,9\right) \land \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(3,\,i,\,7\right) \cdot \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(3,\,j,\,7\right) \bmod 7 = \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(3,\,i + j \bmod 6,\,7\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 46,656 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_lucas\_numbers\_follow\_their\_recurrence\_and\_reach\_their\_named\_values}]
\[ \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(0\right) = 2 \land \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(1\right) = 1 \land \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(5\right) = 11 \land \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(7\right) = 29 \land \forall n \in \{2,\dots,29\},\; \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) + \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(n - 2\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 28 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_lucas\_numbers\_are\_the\_sum\_of\_the\_neighbouring\_fibonaccis}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,28\},\; \mathrm{lucas}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) + \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 28 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_pell\_numbers\_follow\_their\_recurrence\_and\_reach\_their\_named\_values}]
\[ \mathrm{pell}\mathopen{}\left(5\right) = 29 \land \mathrm{pell}\mathopen{}\left(6\right) = 70 \land \forall n \in \{2,\dots,25\},\; \mathrm{pell}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = 2 \cdot \mathrm{pell}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) + \mathrm{pell}\mathopen{}\left(n - 2\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 24 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{consecutive\_farey\_neighbours\_have\_unit\_determinant\_and\_bracket\_their\_mediant}]
\texttt{(List.range ((farey 4).length - 1)).all (fun i => let x := (farey 4).getD i (0, 1); let y := (farey 4).getD (i + 1) (0, 1) x.2 * y.1 == x.1 * y.2 + 1) ∧ (List.range ((farey 6).length - 1)).all (fun i => let x := (farey 6).getD i (0, 1); let y := (farey 6).getD (i + 1) (0, 1) let m := (x.1 + y.1, x.2 + y.2) x.1 * m.2 < m.1 * x.2 \&\& m.1 * y.2 < y.1 * m.2)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_units\_are\_three\_regular\_under\_doubling\_halving\_and\_reflection}]
\[ \mathrm{degreeSet}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right)\right) = [3] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_whole\_ring\_is\_not\_regular\_and\_the\_triad\_is\_why}]
\[ \mathrm{degreeSet}\mathopen{}\left(\{0,\dots,8\}\right) \neq [3] \land \left|\mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\{0,\dots,8\},\,0\right)\right| = 0 \land \forall d \in [3,\,6],\; \left|\mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\{0,\dots,8\},\,d\right)\right| = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,458 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_interior\_and\_exterior\_angles\_are\_n\_straight\_angles}]
\[ \forall n \in \{3,\dots,32\},\; n - 2 \cdot 180 + 360 = n \cdot 180 \land \frac{180 \cdot 3 - 2}{3} = 60 \land \frac{180 \cdot 5 - 2}{5} = 108 \land \frac{180 \cdot 8 - 2}{8} = 135 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 30 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_knight\_has\_exactly\_eight\_leaps\_and\_every\_one\_flips\_the\_colour}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{knightSteps}\right| = 8 \land \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\mathrm{knightSteps}\right)\right| = 8 \land \forall p \in \mathrm{knightSteps},\; p_{1} + p_{2} \bmod 2 = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_golden\_beatty\_identity\_holds\_across\_the\_range}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,40\},\; \mathrm{upperWythoff}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) - \mathrm{lowerWythoff}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = n \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 40 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_wythoff\_sequences\_partition\_the\_integers}]
\texttt{(List.range' 1 50).all (fun m => ((List.range' 1 40).map lowerWythoff).count m + ((List.range' 1 40).map upperWythoff).count m == 1)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 80,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_partition\_and\_bell\_numbers\_count\_what\_they\_claim\_to\_count}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{partitionsOf}\mathopen{}\left(5\right)\right| = 7 \land \left|\mathrm{partitionsOf}\mathopen{}\left(7\right)\right| = 15 \land \left|\mathrm{partitionsOf}\mathopen{}\left(10\right)\right| = 42 \land \forall n \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \left|\mathrm{rgs}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)\right| = \mathrm{bellOf}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \land \mathrm{bellOf}\mathopen{}\left(3\right) = 5 \land \mathrm{bellOf}\mathopen{}\left(4\right) = 15 \land \mathrm{bellOf}\mathopen{}\left(5\right) = 52 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 6 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_unit\_graph\_is\_complete\_bipartite\_between\_the\_two\_tetrahedra}]
\[ \left|\mathrm{tetOf}\mathopen{}\left(1\right)\right| = 3 \land \left|\mathrm{tetOf}\mathopen{}\left(2\right)\right| = 3 \land \forall d \in \mathrm{tetOf}\mathopen{}\left(1\right),\; \left|\mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\,d\right)\right| = 3 \land \forall t \in \mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\,d\right),\; t \in \mathrm{tetOf}\mathopen{}\left(2\right) \land \forall d \in \mathrm{tetOf}\mathopen{}\left(2\right),\; \left|\mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\,d\right)\right| = 3 \land \forall t \in \mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\,d\right),\; t \in \mathrm{tetOf}\mathopen{}\left(1\right) \land \forall d \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; \forall x \in \mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\,d\right),\; \forall y \in \mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\,d\right),\; x = y \lor \lnot y \in \mathrm{nbrs}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\,x\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{elementary\_settles\_its\_range}]
\[ \mathrm{settledHere} = 32 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{rfl} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_interior\_and\_exterior\_angles\_are\_n\_straight\_angles\_for\_every\_n}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, 2 ≤ n → (n - 2) * 180 + 360 = n * 180}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_product\_of\_consecutive\_numbers\_is\_even}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, n * (n + 1) \% 2 = 0}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{eight\_times\_a\_triangular\_number\_plus\_one\_is\_an\_odd\_square\_for\_every\_n}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, 8 * (n * (n + 1) / 2) + 1 = (2 * n + 1) * (2 * n + 1)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_lucas\_numbers\_are\_the\_sum\_of\_the\_neighbouring\_fibonaccis\_for\_every\_n}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, lucas (n + 1) = fib n + fib (n + 2)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  have} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_polygon\_side\_count\_reads\_back\_from\_its\_angle\_sum}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, 2 ≤ n → ((n - 2) * 180 + 360) / 180 = n}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_odd\_square\_one\_more\_than\_eight\_times\_m\_makes\_m\_triangular}]
\texttt{∀ m r : Nat, 8 * m + 1 = r * r → m = r / 2 * (r / 2 + 1) / 2}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_fibonaccis\_are\_a\_fifth\_of\_the\_neighbouring\_lucas\_numbers\_for\_every\_n}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, 5 * fib (n + 1) = lucas n + lucas (n + 2)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{half\_a\_product\_of\_consecutive\_numbers\_multiplies\_back\_for\_every\_n}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, n * (n + 1) / 2 * 2 = n * (n + 1)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{families.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{flt\_all\_primes\_under\_thirty}]
\[ \forall p \in \mathrm{primesUpTo30},\; \forall a \in \{1,\dots,1+p - 1-1\},\; a^{p - 1} \bmod p = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{wilson\_all\_primes\_under\_thirty}]
\[ \forall p \in \mathrm{primesUpTo30},\; \mathrm{fact}\mathopen{}\left(p - 1\right) \bmod p = p - 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{wilson\_fails\_at\_composites}]
\[ \forall n \in \{\, n \in \{4,\dots,23\} \mid \lnot n \in \mathrm{primesUpTo30} \,\},\; \mathrm{fact}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) \bmod n \neq n - 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 20 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{pascal\_rows\_sum\_to\_powers\_of\_two}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,11\},\; \sum \{\, \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(n,\,k\right) \mid k \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \,\} = 2^{n} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 12 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{pascal\_alternating\_sums\_vanish}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,11\},\; \sum \{\, \begin{cases}\mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(n,\,k\right) & \text{if } k \bmod 2 = 0\\ 0 & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \mid k \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \,\} = \sum \{\, \begin{cases}\mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(n,\,k\right) & \text{if } k \bmod 2 = 1\\ 0 & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \mid k \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \,\} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 11 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{totient\_at\_prime\_powers}]
\[ \forall p \in [2,\,3,\,5,\,7],\; \forall k \in \{1,\dots,3\},\; \mathrm{totient}\mathopen{}\left(p^{k}\right) = p^{k} - p^{k - 1} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 12 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{geometric\_series\_all\_bases}]
\[ \forall b \in \{2,\dots,9\},\; \sum \{\, b^{i} \mid i \in \{0,\dots,4\} \,\} \cdot b - 1 = b^{5} - 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 40 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{xor\_is\_parity\_up\_to\_eight\_bits}]
\[ \forall k \in \{1,\dots,8\},\; \forall n \in \{0,\dots,2^{k}-1\},\; \mathrm{popcount}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \bmod 2 = \operatorname{fold}_{\mathrm{acc},\,i \mapsto \mathrm{acc} + n \gg i \bmod 2 \bmod 2}\left(\{0,\dots,k-1\},\, 0\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 8 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{nat\_div\_is\_a\_total\_function\_returning\_zero\_at\_a\_zero\_divisor}]
\[ \frac{7}{0} = 0 \land \frac{0}{0} = 0 \land 7 \bmod 0 = 7 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{division\_identity\_holds\_across\_the\_range}]
\[ \forall d \in \{0,\dots,12\},\; \frac{12}{d} \cdot d + 12 \bmod d = 12 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 13 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{at\_a\_zero\_divisor\_the\_identity\_is\_carried\_by\_the\_remainder}]
\[ \frac{12}{0} = 0 \land 12 \bmod 0 = 12 \land \frac{12}{0} \cdot 0 + 12 \bmod 0 = 12 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{primality\_is\_decided\_across\_the\_range}]
\[ \forall m \in \{2,\dots,18\},\; \mathrm{isPrime}\mathopen{}\left(m\right) = m \in \mathrm{primesUpTo30} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 17 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_units\_are\_cyclic\_at\_exactly\_the\_gauss\_moduli}]
\[ \forall m \in \{2,\dots,18\},\; \mathrm{hasPrimitiveRoot}\mathopen{}\left(m\right) = \mathrm{gaussCyclic}\mathopen{}\left(m\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 17 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{demorgan\_holds\_at\_every\_arity\_to\_eight}]
\[ \forall k \in \{2,\dots,8\},\; \forall n \in \{0,\dots,2^{k}-1\},\; \lnot \forall i \in \{0,\dots,k-1\},\; n \gg i \bmod 2 = 1 = \exists i \in \{0,\dots,k-1\} : n \gg i \bmod 2 = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 7 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{multiplication\_permutes\_z9\_at\_exactly\_the\_units}]
\[ \forall k \in \{1,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{permutesZ9}\mathopen{}\left(k\right) = \mathrm{gcd'}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,9\right) = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 8 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{addition\_generates\_z9\_at\_exactly\_the\_units}]
\[ \forall k \in \{1,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{addGeneratesZ9}\mathopen{}\left(k\right) = \mathrm{gcd'}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,9\right) = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 8 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{an\_inverse\_mod\_nine\_exists\_at\_exactly\_the\_units}]
\[ \forall d \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \exists\,\mathrm{invOf}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) = \mathrm{gcd'}\mathopen{}\left(d,\,9\right) = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 9 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_inverse\_of\_a\_unit\_mod\_nine\_is\_its\_fifth\_power}]
\[ \forall u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; u \cdot \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,5,\,9\right) \bmod 9 = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{power\_sums\_match\_their\_closed\_forms}]
\[ \forall k \in \{1,\dots,5\},\; \forall n \in \{0,\dots,40\},\; \mathrm{powSum}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,n\right) = \mathrm{faulhaber}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 205 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_digital\_root\_is\_invariant\_under\_digit\_reversal}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,99\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,99\},\; \mathrm{digitalRoot}\mathopen{}\left(a \cdot 100 + b\right) = \mathrm{digitalRoot}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{reverseDigits}\mathopen{}\left(a \cdot 100 + b\right)\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{geometric\_series\_across\_bases\_and\_exponents}]
\[ \forall b \in \{2,\dots,12\},\; \forall n \in \{0,\dots,6\},\; \sum \{\, b^{i} \mid i \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \,\} \cdot b - 1 = b^{n + 1} - 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 77 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{totient\_at\_prime\_powers\_through\_thirteen}]
\[ \forall p \in [2,\,3,\,5,\,7,\,11,\,13],\; \forall k \in \{1,\dots,3\},\; \mathrm{totient}\mathopen{}\left(p^{k}\right) = p^{k} - p^{k - 1} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 18 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_powers\_of\_a\_unit\_sum\_to\_zero\_when\_g\_minus\_one\_is\_invertible}]
\[ \forall m \in \{2,\dots,20\},\; \forall g \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(m\right),\; \lnot \mathrm{gcd'}\mathopen{}\left(g - 1,\,m\right) = 1 \lor \mathrm{rootSum}\mathopen{}\left(g,\,m\right) \bmod m = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 19 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{and\_the\_converse\_fails\_at\_six}]
\[ \mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(5,\,6\right) = 2 \land \mathrm{rootSum}\mathopen{}\left(5,\,6\right) \bmod 6 = 0 \land \mathrm{gcd'}\mathopen{}\left(4,\,6\right) = 2 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_first\_n\_odd\_numbers\_sum\_to\_n\_squared}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,200\},\; \sum \{\, 2 \cdot i + 1 \mid i \in \{0,\dots,n-1\} \,\} = n \cdot n \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 201 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_powers\_of\_two\_sum\_to\_one\_less\_than\_the\_next}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,40\},\; \sum \{\, 2^{i} \mid i \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \,\} = 2^{n + 1} - 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 41 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_sums\_of\_triangular\_numbers\_are\_the\_tetrahedral\_numbers}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,100\},\; \sum \{\, \frac{i \cdot i + 1}{2} \mid i \in \{1,\dots,1+n-1\} \,\} = \frac{n \cdot n + 1 \cdot n + 2}{6} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 101 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_squares\_of\_a\_pascal\_row\_sum\_to\_the\_central\_binomial}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,12\},\; \sum \{\, \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(n,\,k\right) \cdot \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(n,\,k\right) \mid k \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\} \,\} = \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot n,\,n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 13 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{polygonal\_closed\_forms\_match\_their\_recurrences}]
\[ \forall s \in \{3,\dots,10\},\; \forall n \in \{0,\dots,50\},\; \frac{s - 2 \cdot n \cdot n + 4 \cdot n - s \cdot n}{2} = \mathrm{polyRec}\mathopen{}\left(s,\,n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 408 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_order\_of\_every\_unit\_mod\_nine\_divides\_six}]
\[ \forall u \in \mathrm{unitsMod}\mathopen{}\left(9\right),\; \mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,9\right) > 0 \land 6 \bmod \mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,9\right) = 0 \land \mathrm{powMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,\mathrm{ordMod}\mathopen{}\left(u,\,9\right),\,9\right) = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_digit\_square\_iteration\_reaches\_one\_or\_four}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,200\},\; \mathrm{iterF}\mathopen{}\left(60,\,n\right) = 1 \lor \mathrm{iterF}\mathopen{}\left(60,\,n\right) = 4 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 200 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_number\_is\_a\_sum\_of\_two\_squares\_exactly\_when\_fermats\_condition\_holds}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,200\},\; \mathrm{isSumOfTwoSquares}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = \mathrm{everyThreeModFourPrimeIsEven}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 200 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_first\_three\_power\_sums\_hold\_to\_two\_hundred}]
\[ \forall k \in \{1,\dots,3\},\; \forall n \in \{0,\dots,200\},\; \mathrm{powSum}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,n\right) = \mathrm{faulhaber}\mathopen{}\left(k,\,n\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 603 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{there\_are\_exactly\_five\_platonic\_solids\_and\_each\_satisfies\_eulers\_formula}]
\texttt{schlafli.length = 5 ∧ schlafli.all (fun x => let d := 2 * x.1 + 2 * x.2 - x.1 * x.2 4 * x.1 \% d == 0 \&\& 2 * x.1 * x.2 \% d == 0 \&\& 4 * x.2 \% d == 0 \&\& 4 * x.1 / d + 4 * x.2 / d == 2 * x.1 * x.2 / d + 2)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{cassinis\_identity\_holds\_across\_the\_range}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,30\},\; \begin{cases}\mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1\right) = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) + 1 & \text{if } n \bmod 2 = 0\\ \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1\right) + 1 = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \cdot \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 30 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_golden\_convergents\_are\_fibonacci\_ratios\_with\_unit\_determinant}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,24\},\; \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{1} = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 2\right) \land \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{2} = \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(n + 1\right) \land \begin{cases}\mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{1} \cdot \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{1} + 1 = \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{1} \cdot \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{2} + \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{2} \cdot \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{2} & \text{if } n \bmod 2 = 0\\ \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{1} \cdot \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{1} = \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{1} \cdot \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{2} + \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{2} \cdot \mathrm{cfPair}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)_{2} + 1 & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 25 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_catalan\_recurrence\_matches\_the\_binomial\_formula}]
\texttt{(List.range 13).all (fun n => (catalanList 12).getD n 0 == choose (2 * n) n / (n + 1))}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 13 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_pisano\_period\_of\_nine\_is\_twenty\_four\_and\_every\_modulus\_to\_twelve\_has\_one}]
\[ \mathrm{pisano}\mathopen{}\left(9\right) = 24 \land \forall m \in \{2,\dots,12\},\; \mathrm{pisano}\mathopen{}\left(m\right) > 0 \land \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{pisano}\mathopen{}\left(m\right)\right) \bmod m = 0 \land \mathrm{fib}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{pisano}\mathopen{}\left(m\right) + 1\right) \bmod m = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 11 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_digit\_sum\_rules\_for\_three\_and\_nine\_hold\_below\_ten\_thousand}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,99\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,99\},\; \mathrm{digitSum}\mathopen{}\left(a \cdot 100 + b\right) \bmod 3 = 0 = a \cdot 100 + b \bmod 3 = 0 \land \mathrm{digitSum}\mathopen{}\left(a \cdot 100 + b\right) \bmod 9 = 0 = a \cdot 100 + b \bmod 9 = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_involutions\_are\_counted\_by\_the\_telephone\_numbers}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,5\},\; \left|\{\, x \in \mathrm{permsN}\mathopen{}\left(\{0,\dots,n-1\}\right) \mid \mathrm{isInvolution}\mathopen{}\left(x\right) \,\}\right| = \mathrm{telephone}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) \]
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{invOf\_is\_an\_inverse\_and\_an\_involution\_on\_the\_units}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{choose\_is\_invariant\_under\_the\_involution\_k\_to\_n\_minus\_k}]
\[ \forall n \in \{0,\dots,12\},\; \forall k \in \{0,\dots,n + 1-1\},\; \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(n,\,k\right) = \mathrm{choose}\mathopen{}\left(n,\,n - k\right) \land n - n - k = k \]
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_ring\_sum\_of\_negatives\_is\_the\_negated\_sum}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{addition\_and\_multiplication\_stay\_inside}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{subtraction\_stays\_inside}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{division\_is\_the\_operation\_that\_leaves}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_root\_is\_a\_single}]

\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{paying\_and\_refunding\_coins\_on\_the\_last\_pair\_round\_trips}]
\[ \forall k \in \{0,\dots,26\},\; 3 \cdot 26 - k + 3 \cdot k = 78 \land 3 \cdot 26 - \mathrm{coins} = 72 \land 72 = 8 \cdot 9 \]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_seal\_divides\_into\_payments\_and\_multiplies\_back}]

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 8 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{sixty\_four\_is\_where\_the\_doubling\_returns}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_orbit\_turns\_once\_per\_budget}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{fibonacci\_gcd\_is\_the\_fibonacci\_of\_the\_gcd}]
\[ \forall m \in \{1,\dots,14\},\; \forall n \in \{1,\dots,14\},\; \mathrm{Families.gcd'}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{Sequences.fib}\mathopen{}\left(m\right),\,\mathrm{Sequences.fib}\mathopen{}\left(n\right)\right) = \mathrm{Sequences.fib}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{Families.gcd'}\mathopen{}\left(m,\,n\right)\right) \]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_mobius\_divisor\_sum\_is\_the\_identity}]

\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_derangement\_recurrence\_holds}]
\[ \forall n \in \{2,\dots,12\},\; \mathrm{derange}\mathopen{}\left(n\right) = n - 1 \cdot \mathrm{derange}\mathopen{}\left(n - 1\right) + \mathrm{derange}\mathopen{}\left(n - 2\right) \land \mathrm{derange}\mathopen{}\left(4\right) = 9 \land \mathrm{derange}\mathopen{}\left(5\right) = 44 \land \mathrm{derange}\mathopen{}\left(6\right) = 265 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 11 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{legendres\_three\_square\_theorem}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{picks\_theorem\_holds\_for\_every\_lattice\_triangle\_in\_the\_four\_grid}]
\[ \forall a \in \mathrm{pts},\; \forall b \in \mathrm{pts},\; \forall c \in \mathrm{pts},\; \mathrm{cross}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b,\,c\right) \le 0 \lor \mathrm{cross}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b,\,c\right) = 2 \cdot \mathrm{interiorCount}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b,\,c\right) + \mathrm{boundaryCount}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b,\,c\right) - 2 \land \lnot \exists a \in \mathrm{pts} : \exists b \in \mathrm{pts} : \exists c \in \mathrm{pts} : \mathrm{cross}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b,\,c\right) > 0 \land \mathrm{cross}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b,\,c\right) = 2 \cdot \mathrm{interiorCount}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b,\,c\right) + \mathrm{boundaryCount}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b,\,c\right) \land \left|\{\, t \in \mathrm{triples} \mid \mathrm{cross}\mathopen{}\left(t_{1},\,t_{2}_{1},\,t_{2}_{2}\right) > 0 \,\}\right| = 1548 \land \left|\{\, t \in \mathrm{triples} \mid \mathrm{cross}\mathopen{}\left(t_{1},\,t_{2}_{1},\,t_{2}_{2}\right) > 0 \land \mathrm{interiorCount}\mathopen{}\left(t_{1},\,t_{2}_{1},\,t_{2}_{2}\right) > 0 \,\}\right| = 516 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{bezouts\_identity\_is\_attained\_and\_no\_smaller\_combination\_exists}]
\[ \forall a \in \{1,\dots,30\},\; \forall b \in \{1,\dots,30\},\; \exists x \in \{0,\dots,30\} : \exists y \in \{0,\dots,30\} : a \cdot x = b \cdot y + \mathrm{Families.gcd'}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) \land \forall a \in \{1,\dots,20\},\; \forall b \in \{1,\dots,20\},\; \forall x \in \{0,\dots,20\},\; \forall y \in \{0,\dots,20\},\; a \cdot x < b \cdot y \lor a \cdot x - b \cdot y \bmod \mathrm{Families.gcd'}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) = 0 \land \mathrm{Families.gcd'}\mathopen{}\left(240,\,46\right) = 2 \land 46 \cdot 47 = 240 \cdot 9 + 2 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 152,568,360,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\end{theorem}

\paragraph{\texttt{energy.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{any\_yield\_below\_its\_cost\_reads\_under\_the\_whole}]
\[ \forall c \in \{1,\dots,60\},\; \forall y \in \{0,\dots,c-1\},\; \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(y,\,c\right) < 100 \land \mathrm{burnYield} < \mathrm{splitCost} \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{burnYield},\,\mathrm{splitCost}\right) = 23 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 60 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_chain\_of\_efficiencies\_can\_only\_lose}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,100\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,100\},\; a \cdot b \le 100 \cdot a \land a \cdot b \le 100 \cdot b \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10,201 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_cost\_per\_litre\_multiplies\_back\_to\_the\_cost}]
\[ \forall l \in \{1,\dots,20\},\; \frac{\mathrm{splitCost}}{l} \cdot l \le \mathrm{splitCost} \land \mathrm{splitCost} < \frac{\mathrm{splitCost}}{l} + 1 \cdot l \land \frac{\mathrm{splitCost}}{\mathrm{waterOut}} = 5777 \land \frac{\mathrm{splitCost}}{\mathrm{waterOut}} > \mathrm{roPerLitre} \cdot 1000 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 20 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_two\_to\_one\_is\_forced\_by\_the\_oxygen}]
\[ \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,2,\,1\right) = \mathrm{true} \land \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(1,\,1,\,1\right) = \mathrm{false} \land \left|\bigcup_{a \in \{1,\dots,9\}} \bigcup_{b \in \{1,\dots,9\}} \{\, c \in \{1,\dots,9\} \mid \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b,\,c\right) \,\}\right| = 4 \land \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(4,\,4,\,2\right) = \mathrm{true} \land \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(6,\,6,\,3\right) = \mathrm{true} \land \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(8,\,8,\,4\right) = \mathrm{true} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 729 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_mass\_balance\_is\_the\_atom\_balance}]
\[ \mathrm{mgH2} = \mathrm{mass}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,0\right) \land \mathrm{mgO2} = \mathrm{mass}\mathopen{}\left(0,\,2\right) \land \mathrm{mgH2O} = \mathrm{mass}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,1\right) \land \forall a \in \{1,\dots,9\},\; \forall b \in \{1,\dots,9\},\; \forall c \in \{1,\dots,9\},\; a \cdot \mathrm{mass}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,1\right) = b \cdot \mathrm{mass}\mathopen{}\left(2,\,0\right) + c \cdot \mathrm{mass}\mathopen{}\left(0,\,2\right) = \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b,\,c\right) \land 2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2O} = 2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2} + \mathrm{mgO2} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 729 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_atom\_table\_decides\_the\_balance}]
\[ \forall a \in \{1,\dots,9\},\; \forall b \in \{1,\dots,9\},\; \forall c \in \{1,\dots,9\},\; a \cdot \mathrm{atomsH2O}_{1} = b \cdot \mathrm{atomsH2}_{1} + c \cdot \mathrm{atomsO2}_{1} \land a \cdot \mathrm{atomsH2O}_{2} = b \cdot \mathrm{atomsH2}_{2} + c \cdot \mathrm{atomsO2}_{2} = \mathrm{balances}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b,\,c\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 729 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_gases\_are\_two\_to\_one\_and\_consume\_each\_other\_exactly}]
\[ 2 \cdot 1 = 2 \land \frac{2 \cdot 100}{3} = 66 \land \frac{1 \cdot 100}{3} = 33 \land \lnot 1 \cdot 1 = 2 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_part\_and\_its\_rest\_read\_back\_the\_whole}]
\[ \forall w \in \{1,\dots,40\},\; \forall a \in \{0,\dots,w + 1-1\},\; \mathrm{per10k}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,w\right) + \mathrm{per10k}\mathopen{}\left(w - a,\,w\right) \le 10000 \land 10000 \le \mathrm{per10k}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,w\right) + \mathrm{per10k}\mathopen{}\left(w - a,\,w\right) + 1 \land \frac{\mathrm{mgO2} \cdot 100}{2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2}} = 793 \land \mathrm{per10k}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2},\,2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2O}\right) = 1119 \land \mathrm{per10k}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{mgO2},\,2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2O}\right) = 8880 \land 2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2O} - 2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2} = \mathrm{mgO2} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 40 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{mass\_is\_conserved\_at\_every\_scale\_so\_the\_loop\_cannot\_make\_water}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,100\},\; 2 \cdot n \cdot \mathrm{mgH2O} = 2 \cdot n \cdot \mathrm{mgH2} + n \cdot \mathrm{mgO2} \land \lnot \forall n \in \{1,\dots,100\},\; n \cdot \mathrm{mgH2O} = n \cdot \mathrm{mgH2} + n \cdot \mathrm{mgO2} \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{one\_litre\_split\_returns\_one\_litre\_burnt}]

\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_gas\_volume\_reads\_back\_its\_moles}]
\[ \forall m \in \{0,\dots,999\},\; \mathrm{molesOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{litresOf}\mathopen{}\left(m\right)\right) \le m \land m \le \mathrm{molesOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{litresOf}\mathopen{}\left(m\right)\right) + 1 \land \mathrm{molH2} = 2 \cdot \mathrm{molO2} \land \mathrm{litresOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{molH2}\right) = 11117 \land \mathrm{litresOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{molO2}\right) = 5558 \land \frac{\mathrm{litresOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{molH2}\right) + \mathrm{litresOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{molO2}\right)}{9} = 1852 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{two\_parts\_in\_three\_is\_two\_thirds\_at\_every\_scale}]
\[ \forall m \in \{1,\dots,500\},\; \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot m,\,3 \cdot m\right) = 66 \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{molH2},\,\mathrm{molH2} + \mathrm{molO2}\right) = 66 \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2},\,2 \cdot \mathrm{mgH2O}\right) = 11 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 500 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_energy\_per\_litre\_multiplies\_back\_to\_the\_energy}]
\[ \forall l \in \{1,\dots,30\},\; \frac{\mathrm{whPerKgH2} \cdot 100}{l} \cdot l \le \mathrm{whPerKgH2} \cdot 100 \land \mathrm{whPerKgH2} \cdot 100 < \frac{\mathrm{whPerKgH2} \cdot 100}{l} + 1 \cdot l \land \frac{\mathrm{whPerKgH2} \cdot 100}{\mathrm{litresOf}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{molH2}\right)} = 299 \land \frac{\mathrm{petrolWhL} \cdot 100}{299} = 3244 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 30 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{compression\_scales\_the\_energy\_per\_litre\_linearly}]
\[ \forall k \in \{0,\dots,99\},\; 2 \cdot \mathrm{whPerLitreAt}\mathopen{}\left(k\right) \le \mathrm{whPerLitreAt}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot k\right) \land \mathrm{whPerLitreAt}\mathopen{}\left(2 \cdot k\right) \le 2 \cdot \mathrm{whPerLitreAt}\mathopen{}\left(k\right) + 1 \land \mathrm{whPerLitreAt}\mathopen{}\left(42\right) = 1398 \land \frac{\mathrm{petrolWhL}}{\mathrm{whPerLitreAt}\mathopen{}\left(42\right)} = 6 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 100 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_ratio\_and\_its\_reciprocal\_multiply\_back\_to\_the\_whole}]
\[ \forall b \in \{1,\dots,40\},\; \forall a \in \{1,\dots,1+b-1\},\; \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) \cdot \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(b,\,a\right) \le 10000 \land 10000 < \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) + 1 \cdot \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(b,\,a\right) + 1 \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{splitCost},\,\mathrm{burnYield}\right) = 433 \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{burnYield},\,\mathrm{splitCost}\right) = 23 \land \frac{9 \cdot 1000}{\frac{\mathrm{burnYield}}{1000}} = 750 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 40 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_loss\_and\_the\_yield\_read\_back\_the\_input}]
\[ \forall c \in \{1,\dots,60\},\; \forall y \in \{0,\dots,c + 1-1\},\; c - y + y = c \land 99 \le \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(c - y,\,c\right) + \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(y,\,c\right) \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(c - y,\,c\right) + \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(y,\,c\right) \le 100 \land \mathrm{splitCost} - \mathrm{burnYield} = 40000 \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{splitCost} - \mathrm{burnYield},\,\mathrm{splitCost}\right) = 76 \land \mathrm{pct}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{burnYield},\,\mathrm{splitCost}\right) = 23 \]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_residue\_ratio\_is\_the\_same\_at\_every\_volume}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_atoms\_read\_back\_from\_the\_mass}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_three\_non\_units\_are\_exactly\_the\_unreachable}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_pattern\_generator\_is\_complete}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{every\_absence\_is\_charged\_to\_exactly\_one\_cause}]

\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_verify\_in\_nanoseconds\_reads\_back\_its\_microseconds}]
\[ \forall u \in \{0,\dots,999\},\; \frac{u \cdot 1000}{1000} = u \land \mathrm{nsPerVerify} = \mathrm{verifyUs} \cdot 1000 \land \frac{\mathrm{nsPerVerify}}{1000} = \mathrm{verifyUs} \land \mathrm{nsPerVerify} > 10000 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 1,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_advantage\_is\_the\_count\_not\_the\_operation}]
\[ 1048576 - 20 = 1048556 \land \frac{1048576}{20} = 52428 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_break\_even\_is\_the\_ratio\_of\_verifications}]

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_width\_is\_the\_ceiling\_of\_128\_bits\_over\_the\_symbol}]
\[ \forall b \in \{1,\dots,8\},\; \mathrm{charsFor}\mathopen{}\left(b\right) \cdot b \ge 128 \land \mathrm{charsFor}\mathopen{}\left(b\right) - 1 \cdot b < 128 \land \mathrm{charsFor}\mathopen{}\left(4\right) = \mathrm{hexChars} \land \mathrm{charsFor}\mathopen{}\left(6\right) = \mathrm{hexbitChars} \land \mathrm{hexbitChars} < \mathrm{hexChars} \land \frac{\mathrm{hexbitChars} \cdot 100}{\mathrm{hexChars}} = 68 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 8 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{hexbits\_are\_slower\_and\_the\_ratio\_multiplies\_back}]
\[ \forall b \in \{1,\dots,20\},\; \forall a \in \{0,\dots,399\},\; \frac{a}{b} \cdot b \le a \land a < \frac{a}{b} + 1 \cdot b \land \mathrm{hexbitMs} > \mathrm{hexMs} \land \frac{\mathrm{hexbitMs} \cdot 10}{\mathrm{hexMs}} = 18 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 8,000 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_nanoseconds\_return\_from\_the\_microseconds\_at\_every\_whole\_count}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, n \% 1000 = 0 → n / 1000 * 1000 = n}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{generated.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{powsum\_zero\_odd\_exponents}]
\[ \forall k \in [1,\,3,\,5,\,7,\,9,\,11,\,13,\,15,\,17],\; \sum \{\, u^{k} \bmod 9 \mid u \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \,\} \bmod 9 = 0 \]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{powsum\_nonzero\_at\_even\_exponents}]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{mulperm\_iff\_unit\_all}]

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 324 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{mulperm\_fails\_at\_the\_triad}]
\[ \lnot \exists k \in [0,\,3,\,6] : \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, k \cdot u \bmod 9 \mid u \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \,\}\right)\right| = 6 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 18 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{addgen\_iff\_coprime\_all}]
\[ \forall k \in \{0,\dots,8\},\; \left|\operatorname{dedup}\left(\{\, k \cdot i \bmod 9 \mid i \in \{0,\dots,8\} \,\}\right)\right| = 9 = k \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 486 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{hasinv\_iff\_unit\_all}]

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 486 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{demorgan\_all\_widths}]
\[ \forall k \in \{2,\dots,8\},\; \forall n \in \{0,\dots,2^{k}-1\},\; \lnot \forall i \in \{0,\dots,k-1\},\; n \gg i \bmod 2 = 1 = \exists i \in \{0,\dots,k-1\} : n \gg i \bmod 2 = 0 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 7 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{power\_sum\_closed\_forms}]
\[ \forall n \in \{1,\dots,30\},\; \sum \{1,\dots,1+n-1\} \cdot 2 = n \cdot n + 1 \land \sum \{\, i \cdot i \mid i \in \{1,\dots,1+n-1\} \,\} \cdot 6 = n \cdot n + 1 \cdot 2 \cdot n + 1 \]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{bouton\_two\_heaps\_lost\_iff\_xor\_zero}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,N-1\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,N-1\},\; \mathrm{isLost}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) = \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) = 0 \]

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{bouton\_lost\_iff\_heaps\_equal}]

\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{lost\_positions\_are\_exactly\_the\_diagonal}]
\[ \left|\{\, p \in \bigcup_{a \in \{0,\dots,N-1\}} \{\, \left(a,\,b\right) \mid b \in \{0,\dots,N-1\} \,\} \mid \mathrm{isLost}\mathopen{}\left(p_{1},\,p_{2}\right) \,\}\right| = N \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{grundy\_of\_a\_single\_heap\_is\_its\_size}]

\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{grundy\_of\_two\_heaps\_is\_the\_xor}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,N-1\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,N-1\},\; \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{grundy1}\mathopen{}\left(a + 1,\,a\right),\,\mathrm{grundy1}\mathopen{}\left(b + 1,\,b\right)\right) = \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{xor\_is\_its\_own\_inverse}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right),\,b\right) = a \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 256 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{xor\_is\_commutative}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) = \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(b,\,a\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 256 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{xor\_zero\_is\_identity}]

\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 32 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_nim\_sum\_is\_a\_gf2\_vector\_addition}]
\[ \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right) = \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(b,\,a\right) \land \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall c \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right),\,c\right) = \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,\mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(b,\,c\right)\right) \land \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \forall b \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,b\right),\,b\right) = a \land \forall a \in \{0,\dots,15\},\; \mathrm{xorN}\mathopen{}\left(a,\,0\right) = a \]
\end{theorem}

\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{a\_trailing\_zero\_is\_a\_leading\_zero\_of\_the\_digits}]
\texttt{∀ m : Nat, 0 < m → digits (10 * m) = 0 :: digits m}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{reversal\_preserves\_the\_digit\_sum\_for\_every\_n}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, digitSum (reverseNum n) = digitSum n}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{the\_fixed\_points\_of\_reversal\_are\_the\_palindromes\_for\_every\_n}]
\texttt{∀ n : Nat, n \% 10 ≠ 0 → (reverseNum n = n ↔ (digits n).reverse = digits n)}
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by
  intro} --- by evaluation; no domain is walked. \hfill$\square$

\paragraph{\texttt{theorems.lean}}

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{universal\_reflection\_involution}]
\[ \forall d \in \{0,\dots,10\},\; \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right)\right) = d \land \left|\{\, d \in \{0,\dots,9\} \mid \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) = d \,\}\right| = 1 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 110 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{universal\_centre\_is\_five}]
\[ \{\, d \in \{0,\dots,9\} \mid \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) = d \,\} = [5] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{universal\_pairs\_sum\_to\_ten}]
\[ \forall d \in \{0,\dots,10\},\; d + \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right) = 10 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 11 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{universal\_reflection\_reverses\_the\_domain}]
\[ \mathrm{refl}[\{0,\dots,10\}] = \operatorname{reverse}\left(\{0,\dots,10\}\right) \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 121 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{universal\_reflection\_is\_not\_an\_involution\_above\_ten}]
\[ \forall d \in \{\, k + 11 \mid k \in \{0,\dots,9\} \,\},\; \lnot \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(\mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(d\right)\right) = d \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{universal\_z9\_reflection\_permutes\_the\_units}]
\[ \{\, 9 - d \bmod 9 \mid d \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \,\} = [8,\,7,\,5,\,4,\,2,\,1] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 36 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{universal\_millennium\_reflection\_escapes\_the\_units}]
\[ \lnot \forall u \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8],\; \mathrm{refl}\mathopen{}\left(u\right) \in [1,\,2,\,4,\,5,\,7,\,8] \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 36 cases. \hfill$\square$

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{universal\_reflection\_is\_the\_vortex\_reflection\_shifted}]
\[ \forall d \in \{0,\dots,9\},\; 10 - d \bmod 9 = 9 - d \bmod 9 + 1 \bmod 9 \]
\end{theorem}
\noindent\emph{Proof.} \texttt{by decide} --- exhausting 10 cases. \hfill$\square$
